data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/Keyboard.tsx","content":"import { FC, memo } from 'react';\nimport { PianoKeyItem, PIANO_KEYS } from '../types';\n\ninterface KeyboardProps {\n  activeNotes?: string[];\n  onNoteStart?: (note: string) => void;\n  onNoteEnd?: (note: string) => void;\n}\n\n// 黒鍵の配置計算（白鍵8鍵：幅12.5%の境界に配置）\nconst getBlackKeyLeftPosition = (note: string): string => {\n  switch (note) {\n    case 'C#':\n      return '8.9%';\n    case 'D#':\n      return '21.4%';\n    case 'F#':\n      return '46.4%';\n    case 'G#':\n      return '58.9%';\n    case 'A#':\n      return '71.4%';\n    default:\n      return '0%';\n  }\n};\n\nconst Keyboard: FC<KeyboardProps> = ({\n  activeNotes = [],\n  onNoteStart,\n  onNoteEnd,\n}) => {\n  const whiteKeys = PIANO_KEYS.filter((key) => !key.isBlack);\n  const blackKeys = PIANO_KEYS.filter((key) => key.isBlack);\n\n  const handlePointerDown = (item: PianoKeyItem, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    onNoteStart?.(item.note);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = (item: PianoKeyItem, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    onNoteEnd?.(item.note);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerLeave = (item: PianoKeyItem, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    onNoteEnd?.(item.note);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"keyboard-outer\">\n      <div className=\"keyboard-container\">\n        {/* 白鍵群の描画 */}\n        {whiteKeys.map((item) => {\n          const isActive = activeNotes.includes(item.note);\n          const buttonClass = isActive\n            ? 'key key-white key-active'\n            : 'key key-white';\n\n          return (\n            <button\n              key={item.note}\n              type=\"button\"\n              className={buttonClass}\n              onPointerDown={(e) => handlePointerDown(item, e)}\n              onPointerUp={(e) => handlePointerUp(item, e)}\n              onPointerLeave={(e) => handlePointerLeave(item, e)}\n              aria-label={`${item.jpName} (${item.keyboardKey})`}\n            >\n              <div className=\"key-label-wrapper\">\n                <span className=\"key-name\">{item.jpName}</span>\n                <span className=\"key-shortcut\">{item.keyboardKey}</span>\n              </div>\n            </button>\n          );\n        })}\n\n        {/* 黒鍵群の絶対配置描画 */}\n        {blackKeys.map((item) => {\n          const isActive = activeNotes.includes(item.note);\n          const buttonClass = isActive\n            ? 'key key-black key-active'\n            : 'key key-black';\n\n          return (\n            <button\n              key={item.note}\n              type=\"button\"\n              className={buttonClass}\n              style={{ left: getBlackKeyLeftPosition(item.note) }}\n              onPointerDown={(e) => handlePointerDown(item, e)}\n              onPointerUp={(e) => handlePointerUp(item, e)}\n              onPointerLeave={(e) => handlePointerLeave(item, e)}\n              aria-label={`${item.jpName} (${item.keyboardKey})`}\n            >\n              <div className=\"key-label-wrapper\">\n                <span className=\"key-name\">{item.jpName}</span>\n                <span className=\"key-shortcut\">{item.keyboardKey}</span>\n              </div>\n            </button>\n          );\n        })}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default memo(Keyboard);","language":"tsx"}

DEMO_SONGS,\n} from './types';\nimport {\n  calculateFrequency,\n  startTone,\n  stopTone,\n  stopAllTones,\n  playTone,\n} from './audio';\nimport Keyboard from './components/Keyboard';\nimport Controls from './components/Controls';\nimport './index.css';\n\ninterface NotePressState {\n  startTime: number;\n}\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [waveform, setWaveform] = useState<WaveformType>('sine');\n  const [volume, setVolume] = useState<number>(0.3);\n  const [octave, setOctave] = useState<number>(4);\n  const [activeNotes, setActiveNotes] = useState<string[]>([]);\n  const [currentNoteDisplay, setCurrentNoteDisplay] = useState<string>('なし');\n\n  // 録音・デモ再生状態\n  const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [recordedNotes, setRecordedNotes] = useState<RecordedNote[]>([]);\n  const [isPlayingRecorded, setIsPlayingRecorded] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [isPlayingDemo, setIsPlayingDemo] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  // 録音追跡用 Ref\n  const recordingStartTimeRef = useRef<number>(0);\n  const notePressMapRef = useRef<Map<string, NotePressState>>(new Map());\n  const activeNotesRef = useRef<string[]>([]);\n  const currentRecordedNotesRef = useRef<RecordedNote[]>([]);\n  const demoTimeoutsRef = useRef<number[]>([]);\n\n  // 最新ステートを Ref に保持\n  const waveformRef = useRef<WaveformType>(waveform);\n  waveformRef.current = waveform;\n  const volumeRef = useRef<number>(volume);\n  volumeRef.current = volume;\n  const octaveRef = useRef<number>(octave);\n  octaveRef.current = octave;\n  const isRecordingRef = useRef<boolean>(isRecording);\n  isRecordingRef.current = isRecording;\n\n  // ノート発音開始処理\n  const handleNoteStart = useCallback((note: string) => {\n    const keyItem = PIANO_KEYS.find((k) => k.note === note);\n    if (!keyItem) return;\n\n    if (!activeNotesRef.current.includes(note)) {\n      activeNotesRef.current = [...activeNotesRef.current, note];\n      setActiveNotes([...activeNotesRef.current]);\n    }\n\n    const freq = calculateFrequency(keyItem.semiToneOffset, octaveRef.current);\n    startTone(note, freq, waveformRef.current, volumeRef.current);\n    setCurrentNoteDisplay(`${keyItem.jpName} (C${octaveRef.current})`);\n\n    // 録音トラッキング\n    if (isRecordingRef.current) {\n      if (!notePressMapRef.current.has(note)) {\n        notePressMapRef.current.set(note, {\n          startTime: Date.now() - recordingStartTimeRef.current,\n        });\n      }\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  // ノート発音終了処理\n  const handleNoteEnd = useCallback((note: string) => {\n    stopTone(note);\n\n    activeNotesRef.current = activeNotesRef.current.filter((n) => n !== note);\n    setActiveNotes([...activeNotesRef.current]);\n\n    if (activeNotesRef.current.length === 0) {\n      setCurrentNoteDisplay('なし');\n    } else {\n      const lastNote = activeNotesRef.current[activeNotesRef.current.length - 1];\n      const item = PIANO_KEYS.find((k) => k.note === lastNote);\n      if (item) {\n        setCurrentNoteDisplay(`${item.jpName} (C${octaveRef.current})`);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // 録音ノート確定\n    if (isRecordingRef.current && notePressMapRef.current.has(note)) {\n      const press = notePressMapRef.current.get(note)!;\n      notePressMapRef.current.delete(note);\n      const currentTime = Date.now() - recordingStartTimeRef.current;\n      const duration = Math.max(100, currentTime - press.startTime);\n\n      currentRecordedNotesRef.current.push({\n        note,\n        time: press.startTime,\n        duration,\n      });\n      setRecordedNotes([...currentRecordedNotesRef.current]);\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  // PCキーボードイベントの監視\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (e.repeat) return;\n      const keyChar = e.key.toUpperCase();\n      const targetItem = PIANO_KEYS.find((k) => k.keyboardKey === keyChar);\n      if (targetItem) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleNoteStart(targetItem.note);\n      }\n    };\n\n    const handleKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      const keyChar = e.key.toUpperCase();\n      const targetItem = PIANO_KEYS.find((k) => k.keyboardKey === keyChar);\n      if (targetItem) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleNoteEnd(targetItem.note);\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    window.addEventListener('keyup', handleKeyUp);\n\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n      window.removeEventListener('keyup', handleKeyUp);\n    };\n  }, [handleNoteStart, handleNoteEnd]);\n\n  // デモ再生のクリア\n  const clearDemoTimeouts = () => {\n    demoTimeoutsRef.current.forEach((t) => clearTimeout(t));\n    demoTimeoutsRef.current = [];\n    stopAllTones();\n    activeNotesRef.current = [];\n    setActiveNotes([]);\n    setIsPlayingDemo(false);\n    setIsPlayingRecorded(false);\n  };\n\n  // デモ曲再生\n  const handlePlayDemo = (demoIndex: number) => {\n    clearDemoTimeouts();\n    const song = DEMO_SONGS[demoIndex];\n    if (!song) return;\n\n    setIsPlayingDemo(true);\n    let maxEndTime = 0;\n\n    song.notes.forEach((item) => {\n      const keyItem = PIANO_KEYS.find((k) => k.note === item.note);\n      if (!keyItem) return;\n\n      const freq = calculateFrequency(keyItem.semiToneOffset, octaveRef.current);\n      const endTime = item.delay + item.duration;\n      if (endTime > maxEndTime) {\n        maxEndTime = endTime;\n      }\n\n      // 発音開始タイマー\n      const startTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        activeNotesRef.current = [...activeNotesRef.current, item.note];\n        setActiveNotes([...activeNotesRef.current]);\n        setCurrentNoteDisplay(`${keyItem.jpName} (C${octaveRef.current})`);\n        playTone(freq, waveformRef.current, volumeRef.current, item.duration);\n      }, item.delay);\n\n      // 視覚的終了タイマー\n      const endTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        activeNotesRef.current = activeNotesRef.current.filter((n) => n !== item.note);\n        setActiveNotes([...activeNotesRef.current]);\n      }, item.delay + item.duration);\n\n      demoTimeoutsRef.current.push(startTimer, endTimer);\n    });\n\n    // 演奏終了タイマー\n    const finishTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {\n      setIsPlayingDemo(false);\n      setCurrentNoteDisplay('なし');\n    }, maxEndTime + 100);\n\n    demoTimeoutsRef.current.push(finishTimer);\n  };\n\n  // 録音トグル\n  const handleToggleRecording = () => {\n    if (isRecording) {\n      // 録音停止\n      setIsRecording(false);\n      isRecordingRef.current = false;\n      // 押しっぱなしだった音の清算\n      const currentTime = Date.now() - recordingStartTimeRef.current;\n      notePressMapRef.current.forEach((press, note) => {\n        currentRecordedNotesRef.current.push({\n          note,\n          time: press.startTime,\n          duration: Math.max(100, currentTime - press.startTime),\n        });\n      });\n      notePressMapRef.current.clear();\n      setRecordedNotes([...currentRecordedNotesRef.current]);\n    } else {\n      // 録音開始\n      clearDemoTimeouts();\n      currentRecordedNotesRef.current = [];\n      setRecordedNotes([]);\n      notePressMapRef.current.clear();\n      recordingStartTimeRef.current = Date.now();\n      setIsRecording(true);\n      isRecordingRef.current = true;\n    }\n  };\n\n recordedNotes.forEach((rn) => {\n      const keyItem = PIANO_KEYS.find((k) => k.note === rn.note);\n      if (!keyItem) return;\n\n      const freq = calculateFrequency(keyItem.semiToneOffset, octaveRef.current);\n      const endTime = rn.time + rn.duration;\n      if (endTime > maxEndTime) {\n        maxEndTime = endTime;\n      }\n\n      const startTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        activeNotesRef.current = [...activeNotesRef.current, rn.note];\n        setActiveNotes([...activeNotesRef.current]);\n        setCurrentNoteDisplay(`${keyItem.jpName} (C${octaveRef.current})`);\n        playTone(freq, waveformRef.current, volumeRef.current, rn.duration);\n      }, rn.time);\n\n      const endTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        activeNotesRef.current = activeNotesRef.current.filter((n) => n !== rn.note);\n        setActiveNotes([...activeNotesRef.current]);\n      }, rn.time + rn.duration);\n\n      demoTimeoutsRef.current.push(startTimer, endTimer);\n    });\n\n    const finishTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {\n      setIsPlayingRecorded(false);\n      setCurrentNoteDisplay('なし');\n    }, maxEndTime + 100);\n\n    demoTimeoutsRef.current.push(finishTimer);\n  };\n\n  // アンマウント時の停止処理\n  useEffect(() => {\n    return () => {\n      clearDemoTimeouts();\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  return (\n    <div onToggleRecording={handleToggleRecording}\n          onPlayRecording={handlePlayRecording}\n          onPlayDemo={handlePlayDemo}\n          onStopDemo={clearDemoTimeouts}\n        />\n\n        {/* 鍵盤エリア */}\n        <Keyboard\n          activeNotes={activeNotes}\n          onNoteStart={handleNoteStart}\n          onNoteEnd={handleNoteEnd}\n        />\n\n        {/* ステータスバー */}\n        <div data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/Keyboard.tsx","content":"import { FC, memo } from 'react';\nimport { PianoKeyItem, PIANO_KEYS } from '../types';\n\ninterface KeyboardProps {\n  activeNotes?: string[];\n  onNoteStart?: (note: string) => void;\n  onNoteEnd?: (note: string) => void;\n}\n\n// 黒鍵の配置オフセット定義（白鍵全8鍵に対する各黒鍵の水平左位置 %）\nconst BLACK_KEY_POSITIONS: Record<string, string> = {\n  'C#': '8.9%',\n  'D#': '21.4%',\n  'F#': '46.4%',\n  'G#': '58.9%',\n  'A#': '71.4%',\n};\n\nconst getBlackKeyLeftPosition = (note: string): string => {\n  return BLACK_KEY_POSITIONS[note] || '0%';\n};\n\nconst Keyboard: FC<KeyboardProps> = ({\n  activeNotes = [],\n  onNoteStart,\n  onNoteEnd,\n}) => {\n  const whiteKeys = PIANO_KEYS.filter((key) => !key.isBlack);\n  const blackKeys = PIANO_KEYS.filter((key) => key.isBlack);\n\n  const handlePointerDown = (item: PianoKeyItem, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    onNoteStart?.(item.note);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerUp = (item: PianoKeyItem, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    onNoteEnd?.(item.note);\n  };\n\n  const handlePointerLeave = (item: PianoKeyItem, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    onNoteEnd?.(item.note);\n  };\n\n  // ドラッグ操作（グリッサンド）で鍵盤に入った際、主ボタンが押されていれば発音\n  const handlePointerEnter = (item: PianoKeyItem, e: React.PointerEvent) => {\n    if (e.buttons === 1) {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      onNoteStart?.(item.note);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"keyboard-outer\">\n      <div className=\"keyboard-container\">\n        {/* 白鍵群の描画 */}\n        {whiteKeys.map((item) => {\n          const isActive = activeNotes.includes(item.note);\n          const buttonClass = isActive\n            ? 'key key-white key-active'\n            : 'key key-white';\n\n          return (\n            <button\n              key={item.note}\n              type=\"button\"\n              className={buttonClass}\n              aria-pressed={isActive}\n              onPointerDown={(e) => handlePointerDown(item, e)}\n              onPointerUp={(e) => handlePointerUp(item, e)}\n              onPointerLeave={(e) => handlePointerLeave(item, e)}\n              onPointerEnter={(e) => handlePointerEnter(item, e)}\n              aria-label={`${item.jpName} (${item.keyboardKey})`}\n            >\n              <div className=\"key-label-wrapper\">\n                <span className=\"key-name\">{item.jpName}</span>\n                <span className=\"key-shortcut\">{item.keyboardKey}</span>\n              </div>\n            </button>\n          );\n        })}\n\n        {/* 黒鍵群の絶対配置描画 */}\n        {blackKeys.map((item) => {\n          const isActive = activeNotes.includes(item.note);\n          const buttonClass = isActive\n            ? 'key key-black key-active'\n            : 'key key-black';\n\n          return (\n            <button\n              key={item.note}\n              type=\"button\"\n              className={buttonClass}\n              style={{ left: getBlackKeyLeftPosition(item.note) }}\n              aria-pressed={isActive}\n              onPointerDown={(e) => handlePointerDown(item, e)}\n              onPointerUp={(e) => handlePointerUp(item, e)}\n              onPointerLeave={(e) => handlePointerLeave(item, e)}\n              onPointerEnter={(e) => handlePointerEnter(item, e)}\n              aria-label={`${item.jpName} (${item.keyboardKey})`}\n            >\n              <div className=\"key-label-wrapper\">\n                <span className=\"key-name\">{item.jpName}</span>\n                <span className=\"key-shortcut\">{item.keyboardKey}</span>\n              </div>\n            </button>\n          );\n        })}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default memo(Keyboard);","language":"tsx"}

'録音を停止'\n    : '演奏を録音';\n\n  // 録音再生ボタン無効化理由\n  const playDisabled =\n    !hasRecordedNotes || isRecording || isPlayingRecorded || isPlayingDemo;\n  const playTitle = !hasRecordedNotes\n    ? '録音データがありません'\n    : isRecording\n    ? '録音中は再生できません'\n    : isPlayingDemo\n    ? 'デモ演奏中は再生できません'\n    : isPlayingRecorded\n    ? '再生中...'\n    : '録音した演奏を再生';\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"controls-panel\">\n      {/* 音色（波形）選択 */}\n      <div className=\"control-group\">\n        <span className=\"control-label\">音色</span>\n        <div className=\"waveform-group\">\n          {WAVEFORMS.map((wf) => {\n            const isActive = waveform === wf.type;\n            const btnClass = isActive\n              ? className={btnClass}\n                onClick={() => onWaveformChange?.(wf.type)}\n              >\n                {wf.label}\n              </button>\n            );\n          })}\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      {/* 音量調整 */}\n      <div className=\"control-group\">\n        <span className=\"control-label\">音量 ({Math.round(volume * 100)}%)</span>\n        <div className=\"slider-control\">\n          <input\n            type=\"range\"\n            min={MIN_VOLUME}\n            max={MAX_VOLUME}\n            step={VOLUME_STEP}\n            value={volume}\n            onChange={handleVolumeInput}\n            className=\"volume-slider\"\n            aria-label=\"音量\"\n            aria-valuemin={MIN_VOLUME}\n            aria-valuemax={MAX_VOLUME}\n oscillator: osc, gainNode });\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error('Failed to start audio tone:', err);\n  }\n};\n\nexport const stopTone = (note: string): void => {\n  const voice = activeVoices.get(note);\n  if (!voice) return;\n\n  activeVoices.delete(note);\n\n  try {\n    const ctx = getAudioContext();\n    const { oscillator, gainNode } = voice;\n    const releaseTime = 0.08;\n\n    gainNode.gain.cancelScheduledValues(ctx.currentTime);\n    gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(gainNode.gain.value, ctx.currentTime);\n    gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, ctx.currentTime + releaseTime);\n\n    oscillator.stop(ctx.currentTime + releaseTime);\n    oscillator.onended = () => {\n      try {\n        oscillator.disconnect();\n        gainNode.disconnect();\n      } catch {\n        // 解放時の例外は無視\n      }\n    };\n  } catch {\n    // 停止時の例外は無視\n  }\n};\n\nexport const playTone = (\n  frequency: number,\n  waveform: WaveformType,\n  volume: number,\n  durationMs: number\n): Promise<void> => {\n  return new Promise((resolve) => {\n    try {\n      const ctx = getAudioContext();\n      if (ctx.state === 'suspended') {\n        ctx.resume().catch(() => {});\n      }\n\n      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();\n      const gainNode = ctx.createGain();\n\n      osc.type = waveform;\n      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, ctx.currentTime);\n\n      const targetGain = Math.max(0.0001, Math.min(1, volume));\n      const durationSec = durationMs / 1000;\n      const attackSec = 0.015;\n      const releaseSec = Math.min(0.1, durationSec * 0.3);\n      const sustainSec = Math.max(0, durationSec - attackSec - releaseSec);\n\n      gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, ctx.currentTime);\n      gainNode.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(targetGain, ctx.currentTime + attackSec);\n      gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(targetGain, ctx.currentTime + attackSec + sustainSec);\n      gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, ctx.currentTime + durationSec);\n\n      osc.connect(gainNode);\n      gainNode.connect(ctx.destination);\n\n      osc.onended = () => {\n        try {\n          osc.disconnect();\n          gainNode.disconnect();\n        } catch {\n          // 解放時の例外は無視\n        }\n        resolve();\n      };\n\n      osc.start(ctx.currentTime);\n      osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + durationSec);\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error('Failed to play tone:', err);\n      resolve();\n    }\n  });\n};\n\nexport const stopAllTones = (): void => {\n  const notes = Array.from(activeVoices.keys());\n  for (let i = 0; i < notes.length; i++) {\n    stopTone(notes[i]);\n  }\n};","language":"typescript"}

data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/App.tsx","content":"import { useState, useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';\nimport {\n  WaveformType,\n  RecordedNote,\n  PIANO_KEYS,\n  DEMO_SONGS,\n} from './types';\nimport {\n  calculateFrequency,\n  startTone,\n  stopTone,\n  stopAllTones,\n  playTone,\n} from './audio';\nimport Keyboard from './components/Keyboard';\nimport Controls from './components/Controls';\nimport './index.css';\n\ninterface NotePressState {\n  startTime: number;\n}\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [waveform, setWaveform] = useState<WaveformType>('sine');\n  const [volume, setVolume] = useState<number>(0.3);\n  const [octave, setOctave] = useState<number>(4);\n  const [activeNotes, setActiveNotes] = useState<string[]>([]);\n  const [currentNoteDisplay, setCurrentNoteDisplay] = useState<string>('なし');\n\n  // 録音・デモ再生状態\n  const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [recordedNotes, setRecordedNotes] = useState<RecordedNote[]>([]);\n  const [isPlayingRecorded, setIsPlayingRecorded] = useState<boolean>(false);\n  const [isPlayingDemo, setIsPlayingDemo] = useState<boolean>(false);\n\n  // 録音追跡用 Ref\n  const recordingStartTimeRef = useRef<number>(0);\n  const notePressMapRef = useRef<Map<string, NotePressState>>(new Map());\n  const activeNotesRef = useRef<string[]>([]);\n  const currentRecordedNotesRef = useRef<RecordedNote[]>([]);\n  const demoTimeoutsRef = useRef<number[]>([]);\n\n  // 最新ステートを Ref に保持\n  const waveformRef = useRef<WaveformType>(waveform);\n  waveformRef.current = waveform;\n  const volumeRef = useRef<number>(volume);\n  volumeRef.current = volume;\n  const octaveRef = useRef<number>(octave);\n  octaveRef.current = octave;\n  const isRecordingRef = useRef<boolean>(isRecording);\n  isRecordingRef.current = isRecording;\n\n  // オクターブ変更ハンドラ（音高不整合防止のため発音中ノートをリセット）\n  const handleOctaveChange = useCallback((newOctave: number) => {\n    stopAllTones();\n    activeNotesRef.current = [];\n    setActiveNotes([]);\n    setCurrentNoteDisplay('なし');\n    setOctave(newOctave);\n  }, []);\n\n  // ノート発音開始処理\n  const handleNoteStart = useCallback((note: string) => {\n    const keyItem = PIANO_KEYS.find((k) => k.note === note);\n    if (!keyItem) return;\n\n    if (!activeNotesRef.current.includes(note)) {\n      activeNotesRef.current = [...activeNotesRef.current, note];\n      setActiveNotes([...activeNotesRef.current]);\n    }\n\n    const freq = calculateFrequency(keyItem.semiToneOffset, octaveRef.current);\n    startTone(note, freq, waveformRef.current, volumeRef.current);\n    setCurrentNoteDisplay(`${keyItem.jpName} (C${octaveRef.current})`);\n\n    // 録音トラッキング\n    if (isRecordingRef.current) {\n      if (!notePressMapRef.current.has(note)) {\n        notePressMapRef.current.set(note, {\n          startTime: Date.now() - recordingStartTimeRef.current,\n        });\n      }\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  // ノート発音終了処理\n  const handleNoteEnd = useCallback((note: string) => {\n    stopTone(note);\n\n    activeNotesRef.current = activeNotesRef.current.filter((n) => n !== note);\n    setActiveNotes([...activeNotesRef.current]);\n\n    if (activeNotesRef.current.length === 0) {\n      setCurrentNoteDisplay('なし');\n    } else {\n      const lastNote = activeNotesRef.current[activeNotesRef.current.length - 1];\n      const item = PIANO_KEYS.find((k) => k.note === lastNote);\n      if (item) {\n        setCurrentNoteDisplay(`${item.jpName} (C${octaveRef.current})`);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // 録音ノート確定\n    if (isRecordingRef.current && notePressMapRef.current.has(note)) {\n      const press = notePressMapRef.current.get(note)!;\n      notePressMapRef.current.delete(note);\n      const currentTime = Date.now() - recordingStartTimeRef.current;\n      const duration = Math.max(100, currentTime - press.startTime);\n\n      currentRecordedNotesRef.current.push({\n        note,\n        time: press.startTime,\n        duration,\n      });\n      setRecordedNotes([...currentRecordedNotesRef.current]);\n    }\n  }, []);\n\n  // PCキーボードイベントの監視\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (e.repeat || e.ctrlKey || e.altKey || e.metaKey) return;\n      const keyChar = e.key.toUpperCase();\n      const targetItem = PIANO_KEYS.find((k) => k.keyboardKey === keyChar);\n      if (targetItem) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleNoteStart(targetItem.note);\n      }\n    };\n\n    const handleKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      const keyChar = e.key.toUpperCase();\n      const targetItem = PIANO_KEYS.find((k) => k.keyboardKey === keyChar);\n      if (targetItem) {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        handleNoteEnd(targetItem.note);\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    window.addEventListener('keyup', handleKeyUp);\n\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n      window.removeEventListener('keyup', handleKeyUp);\n    };\n  }, [handleNoteStart, handleNoteEnd]);\n\n  // デモ再生のクリア\n  const clearDemoTimeouts = () => {\n    demoTimeoutsRef.current.forEach((t) => clearTimeout(t));\n    demoTimeoutsRef.current = [];\n    stopAllTones();\n    activeNotesRef.current = [];\n    setActiveNotes([]);\n    setIsPlayingDemo(false);\n    setIsPlayingRecorded(false);\n  };\n\n  // デモ曲再生\n  const handlePlayDemo = (demoIndex: number) => {\n    clearDemoTimeouts();\n    const song = DEMO_SONGS[demoIndex];\n    if (!song) return;\n\n    setIsPlayingDemo(true);\n    let maxEndTime = 0;\n\n    song.notes.forEach((item) => {\n      const keyItem = PIANO_KEYS.find((k) => k.note === item.note);\n      if (!keyItem) return;\n\n      const freq = calculateFrequency(keyItem.semiToneOffset, octaveRef.current);\n      const endTime = item.delay + item.duration;\n      if (endTime > maxEndTime) {\n        maxEndTime = endTime;\n      }\n\n      // 発音開始タイマー\n      const startTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        activeNotesRef.current = [...activeNotesRef.current, item.note];\n        setActiveNotes([...activeNotesRef.current]);\n        setCurrentNoteDisplay(`${keyItem.jpName} (C${octaveRef.current})`);\n        playTone(freq, waveformRef.current, volumeRef.current, item.duration);\n      }, item.delay);\n\n      // 視覚的終了タイマー\n      const endTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        activeNotesRef.current = activeNotesRef.current.filter((n) => n !== item.note);\n        setActiveNotes([...activeNotesRef.current]);\n      }, item.delay + item.duration);\n\n      demoTimeoutsRef.current.push(startTimer, endTimer);\n    });\n\n    // 演奏終了タイマー\n    const finishTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {\n      setIsPlayingDemo(false);\n      setCurrentNoteDisplay('なし');\n    }, maxEndTime + 100);\n\n    demoTimeoutsRef.current.push(finishTimer);\n  };\n\n  // 録音トグル\n  const handleToggleRecording = () => {\n    if (isRecording) {\n      // 録音停止\n      setIsRecording(false);\n      isRecordingRef.current = false;\n      // 押しっぱなしだった音の清算\n      const currentTime = Date.now() - recordingStartTimeRef.current;\n      notePressMapRef.current.forEach((press, note) => {\n        currentRecordedNotesRef.current.push({\n          note,\n          time: press.startTime,\n          duration: Math.max(100, currentTime - press.startTime),\n        });\n      });\n      notePressMapRef.current.clear();\n      setRecordedNotes([...currentRecordedNotesRef.current]);\n    } else {\n      // 録音開始\n      clearDemoTimeouts();\n      currentRecordedNotesRef.current = [];\n      setRecordedNotes([]);\n      notePressMapRef.current.clear();\n      recordingStartTimeRef.current = Date.now();\n      setIsRecording(true);\n      isRecordingRef.current = true;\n    }\n  };\n\n  // 録音演奏の再生\n  const handlePlayRecording = () => {\n    if (recordedNotes.length === 0) return;\n    clearDemoTimeouts();\n    setIsPlayingRecorded(true);\n\n    let maxEndTime = 0;\n\n    recordedNotes.forEach((rn) => {\n      const keyItem = PIANO_KEYS.find((k) => k.note === rn.note);\n      if (!keyItem) return;\n\n      const freq = calculateFrequency(keyItem.semiToneOffset, octaveRef.current);\n      const endTime = rn.time + rn.duration;\n      if (endTime > maxEndTime) {\n        maxEndTime = endTime;\n      }\n\n      const startTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        activeNotesRef.current = [...activeNotesRef.current, rn.note];\n        setActiveNotes([...activeNotesRef.current]);\n        setCurrentNoteDisplay(`${keyItem.jpName} (C${octaveRef.current})`);\n        playTone(freq, waveformRef.current, volumeRef.current, rn.duration);\n      }, rn.time);\n\n      const endTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {\n        activeNotesRef.current = activeNotesRef.current.filter((n) => n !== rn.note);\n        setActiveNotes([...activeNotesRef.current]);\n      }, rn.time + rn.duration);\n\n      demoTimeoutsRef.current.push(startTimer, endTimer);\n    });\n\n    const finishTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {\n      setIsPlayingRecorded(false);\n      setCurrentNoteDisplay('なし');\n    }, maxEndTime + 100);\n\n    demoTimeoutsRef.current.push(finishTimer);\n  };\n\n  // アンマウント時の停止処理\n  useEffect(() => {\n    return () => {\n      clearDemoTimeouts();\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"piano-app\">\n      {/* タイトル部 */}\n      <header className=\"piano-header\">\n        <h1 onVolumeChange={setVolume}\n          onOctaveChange={handleOctaveChange}\n          onToggleRecording={handleToggleRecording}\n          onPlayRecording={handlePlayRecording}\n          onPlayDemo={handlePlayDemo}\n          onStopDemo={clearDemoTimeouts}\n        />\n\n        {/* 鍵盤エリア */}\n        <Keyboard\n          activeNotes={activeNotes}\n          onNoteStart={handleNoteStart}\n          onNoteEnd={handleNoteEnd}\n        />\n\n        {/* ステータスバー */}\n        <div className=\"status-bar\">\n          <div className=\"current-note-display\">\n audioCtx: AudioContext | null = null;\n\ninterface ActiveVoice {\n  oscillator: OscillatorNode;\n  gainNode: GainNode;\n}\n\nconst activeVoices = new Map<string, ActiveVoice>();\n\nexport const getAudioContext = (): AudioContext => {\n  if (!audioCtx) {\n    const AudioContextClass =\n      window.AudioContext ||\n      (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;\n    audioCtx = new AudioContextClass();\n  }\n  if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') {\n    audioCtx.resume().catch(() => {});\n  }\n  return audioCtx;\n};\n\nexport const calculateFrequency = (semiToneOffset: number, octave: number): number => {\n  const a4Frequency = 440;\n  const a4OffsetFromC4 = 9;\n  const totalOffsetFromA4 = semiToneOffset - a4OffsetFromC4 + (octave - 4) * 12;\n  return a4Frequency * Math.pow(2, totalOffsetFromA4 / 12);\n};\n\nexport const startTone = (\n  note: string,\n  frequency: number,\n  waveform: WaveformType,\n  volume: number\n): void => {\n  try {\n    const ctx = getAudioContext();\n    if (ctx.state === 'suspended') {\n      ctx.resume().catch(() => {});\n    }\n\n    stopTone(note);\n\n    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();\n    const gainNode = ctx.createGain();\n\n    osc.type = waveform;\n    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, ctx.currentTime);\n\n    const targetGain = Math.max(0.0001, Math.min(1, volume));\n    gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, ctx.currentTime);\n    gainNode.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(targetGain, ctx.currentTime + 0.015);\n\n    osc.connect(gainNode);\n    gainNode.connect(ctx.destination);\n\n    osc.start();\n    activeVoices.set(note, { oscillator: osc, gainNode });\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.error('Failed to start audio tone:', err);\n  }\n};\n\nexport const stopTone = (note: string): void => {\n  const voice = activeVoices.get(note);\n  if (!voice) return;\n\n  activeVoices.delete(note);\n\n  try {\n    const ctx = getAudioContext();\n    const { oscillator, gainNode } = voice;\n    const releaseTime = 0.08;\n\n    gainNode.gain.cancelScheduledValues(ctx.currentTime);\n    gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(gainNode.gain.value, ctx.currentTime);\n    gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, ctx.currentTime + releaseTime);\n\n    oscillator.stop(ctx.currentTime + releaseTime);\n    oscillator.onended = () => {\n      try {\n        oscillator.disconnect();\n        gainNode.disconnect();\n      } catch {\n        // 解放時の例外は無視\n      }\n    };\n  } catch {\n    // 停止時の例外は無視\n  }\n};\n\nexport const playTone = (\n  frequency: number,\n  waveform: WaveformType,\n  volume: number,\n  durationMs: number\n): Promise<void> => {\n  return new Promise((resolve) => {\n    try {\n      const ctx = getAudioContext();\n      if (ctx.state === 'suspended') {\n        ctx.resume().catch(() => {});\n      }\n\n      const osc = ctx.createOscillator();\n      const gainNode = ctx.createGain();\n\n      osc.type = waveform;\n      osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency, ctx.currentTime);\n\n      const targetGain = Math.max(0.0001, Math.min(1, volume));\n      const durationSec = durationMs / 1000;\n      const attackSec = 0.015;\n      const releaseSec = Math.min(0.1, durationSec * 0.3);\n      const sustainSec = Math.max(0, durationSec - attackSec - releaseSec);\n\n      gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, ctx.currentTime);\n      gainNode.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(targetGain, ctx.currentTime + attackSec);\n      gainNode.gain.setValueAtTime(targetGain, ctx.currentTime + attackSec + sustainSec);\n      gainNode.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, ctx.currentTime + durationSec);\n\n      osc.connect(gainNode);\n      gainNode.connect(ctx.destination);\n\n      osc.onended = () => {\n        try {\n          osc.disconnect();\n          gainNode.disconnect();\n        } catch {\n          // 解放時の例外は無視\n        }\n        resolve();\n      };\n\n      osc.start(ctx.currentTime);\n      osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + durationSec);\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error('Failed to play tone:', err);\n      resolve();\n    }\n  });\n};\n\nexport const stopAllTones = (): void => {\n  const notes = Array.from(activeVoices.keys());\n  for (let i = 0; i < notes.length; i++) {\n startTime: number;\n}\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [waveform, setWaveform] = useState<WaveformType>('sine');\n  const [volume, setVolume] = useState<number>(0.3);\n  const [octave, setOctave] = setActiveNotes([]);\n    setCurrentNoteDisplay('なし');\n    setOctave(newOctave);\n  }, []);\n\n  // ノート発音開始処理\n  const handleNoteStart = useCallback((note: string) => {\n    const keyItem = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n      if (e.repeat || e.ctrlKey || e.altKey || e.metaKey) return;\n      const keyChar = e.key.toUpperCase();\n      const targetItem = PIANO_KEYS.find((k) => k.keyboardKey  }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    window.addEventListener('keyup', handleKeyUp);\n\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('keydown', demoTimeoutsRef.current.forEach((t) => clearTimeout(t));\n    demoTimeoutsRef.current = [];\n    stopAllTones();\n    activeNotesRef.current = [];\n    setActiveNotes([]);\n    setIsPlayingDemo(false);\n    setIsPlayingRecorded(false);\n  };\n\n  // デモ曲再生\n  const handlePlayDemo = (demoIndex: number) => {\n    clearDemoTimeouts();\n    const song = DEMO_SONGS[demoIndex];\n    if (!song) return;\n\n    setIsPlayingDemo(true);\n    let maxEndTime = 0;\n\n    song.notes.forEach((item) => {\n      const keyItem = PIANO_KEYS.find((k) => k.note === item.note);\n      if const finishTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {\n      setIsPlayingDemo(false);\n      setCurrentNoteDisplay('なし');\n    }, maxEndTime + 100);\n\n    demoTimeoutsRef.current.push(finishTimer);\n  };\n\n  // 録音トグル\n  const handleToggleRecording = () => {\n    if (isRecording) {\n      // 録音停止\n      setIsRecording(false);\n      isRecordingRef.current = false;\n      // 押しっぱなしだった音の清算\n      const setRecordedNotes([...currentRecordedNotesRef.current]);\n    } else {\n      // 録音開始\n      clearDemoTimeouts();\n      currentRecordedNotesRef.current = [];\n      setRecordedNotes([]);\n      notePressMapRef.current.clear();\n      recordingStartTimeRef.current = Date.now();\n      setIsRecording(true);\n      isRecordingRef.current = true;\n    }\n  };\n\n  // 録音演奏の再生\n  const handlePlayRecording = () => {\n    if (recordedNotes.length === 0) return;\n    clearDemoTimeouts();\n    setIsPlayingRecorded(true);\n\n    let maxEndTime = 0;\n\n    recordedNotes.forEach((rn) => {\n      const keyItem = PIANO_KEYS.find((k) => k.note === rn.note);\n      if (!keyItem) return;\n\n      const freq = calculateFrequency(keyItem.semiToneOffset, octaveRef.current);\n      const window.setTimeout(() => {\n      setIsPlayingRecorded(false);\n      setCurrentNoteDisplay('なし');\n    }, maxEndTime + 100);\n\n    demoTimeoutsRef.current.push(finishTimer);\n  };\n\n  // アンマウント時の停止処理\n  useEffect(() => {\n    return () => {\n      clearDemoTimeouts();\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"piano-app\">\n      {/* タイトル部 */}\n      <header </header>\n\n      {/* ピアノ本体外郭 */}\n      <main className=\"piano-board\">\n        {/* 操作パネル */}\n        <Controls\n          waveform={waveform}\n          volume={volume}\n          octave={octave}\n          isRecording={isRecording}\n          isPlayingRecorded={isPlayingRecorded}\n          hasRecordedNotes={recordedNotes.length > 0}\n          isPlayingDemo={isPlayingDemo}\n          onWaveformChange={setWaveform}\n          onVolumeChange={setVolume}\n          onOctaveChange={handleOctaveChange}\n          onToggleRecording={handleToggleRecording}\n          onPlayRecording={handlePlayRecording}\n          onPlayDemo={handlePlayDemo}\n          onStopDemo={clearDemoTimeouts}\n        />\n\n        {/* 鍵盤エリア */}\n        <Keyboard\n
